Let's look at SOTA LP more. It is an iterative method known as "descent on L∞ norm of product via maximum volume in polar body". We can implement a simpler version:
- Normalize a sum=1.
- M = max q.
- Find g that maximizes sum g subject to a*g ≤ M (full length maybe) and g≥0. This LP can be solved approximately by solving dual:
Primal: max 1^T g. Constraints: (T_k g)_k ≤ M for k=0..2n-2; g≥0.
Dual: min M Σ y_k + λ 1^T? Let's derive. Max 1^T g s.t. A g ≤ M 1, g ≥0. Dual variables y≥0: min M Σ y_k subject to A^T y ≥ 1. Here A is convolution operator with f? A_k,i = f_{k-i} if valid. So dual: minimize M ∑ y_k subject to ∑_{k} f_{k-i} y_k ≥1 for all i, y≥0. This is a linear program with 2n-1 variables, n constraints. It might be easier: need find nonnegative measures y on sum bins such that their convolution with f covers 1, minimize mass. This is like designing a counter distribution. Extreme points have active constraints. We can solve dual with scipy linprog variables ~11163, constraints 5582. That's still large but maybe manageable with HiGHS sparse? A^T is sparse? Each y_k affects variables i in [0,n-1] where i and k-i valid: at most n, total dense ~n*2n=60M. But f can be smooth; not sparse. But can solve via cutting plane: maintain subset of constraints (active i). We can find violating variable by min_i ∑ y_k f_{k-i}. If all ≥1, optimal for active? In dual, adding constraints can increase mass. We can use column? Alternatively, primal active constraints method easier: maintain active bins K where a*g=M, solve LP with |K| constraints. The optimal g will have many zeros if K small. K can be ~n? But maybe active set small for smooth functions.

A local search approach:
- Represent a as nonnegative float array length n, sum=1.
- Objective q = convolution; max M.
- Subgradient descent: choose distribution over active bins (q_k near max), gradient g_i = Σ_{active k} r_k a_{k-i}. Update a *= exp(-η g) / normalize, or a += -η (g - mean g) with clipping. This is like gradient of max with randomized active bins.
- To escape local minima, periodically add random perturbations (geometric, sinusoidal) and heat-anneal.

Algorithm idea:
1. For several n (e.g., 512, 1024, 2048), generate initial sequences:

What constructions yield ratio 1.5? Maybe taking a_i as coefficients of a power `(1+x+...+x^{m-1})^p`? Let's analyze powers.

Let P_n = (1+x+...+x^{m-1})^d. Coefficients are number of compositions; sum = m^d. Square P_n² = (1+...+x^{m-1})^{2d}, coefficients are m-convolution; max around center. Score for P = 2n max / sum². Let's design `iterate_lse(a, beta, eta)` more carefully:
- q = conv_fft(a) real, length 2n-1.
- M = max(q). If M <= target maybe break.
- To avoid overflow in exp, set z = q - max(q); choose beta such that beta*M maybe 10? If M ~target/n ~1e-4, beta=1e5. z max0, min maybe -0.0001; beta*z in [-10,0]. exp safe. - grad = 2 * corr(r,a). Since r sum1, grad sum? Not needed.
- `g = grad - np.mean(grad)` (Lagrange for sum constraint). Update log: `delta = -eta * g / scale_g`? Need choose eta dimensionless. g magnitude: average a ~1/n=0.00018; grad ~2*(sum r a over ~n) maybe ~1/n. Mean similar. Difference scale maybe. If eta=1, exponent changes O(1). We can set `norm_g = np.linalg.norm(g)`; if >0, `step = eta * g / max(norm_g, 1e-12)`. Then a2 = a * exp(-step) maybe? If divide by norm, eta is log-change scale. But if norm small, skip. Better: `step = eta * (g / np.std(g))`? For random, changes a by multiples of std. Let's use `g_centered = g - np.mean(g)`. `g_scale = np.sqrt(np.mean(g_centered**2))`. If g_scale small, random kick. `step = eta * g_centered / g_scale`; clip step to [-5,5]. Then a2 = a * exp(-step). Normalize.
- Line search: Accept if internal M2 < M. Because of FFT errors, use a tiny margin. If fail, eta *=0.5; if fail many, random kick. If success, eta *=1.1 up to max_eta (e.g., 5). This is a multiplicative gradient descent with normalized gradient, robust.

However, this update does not explicitly target exact max but smoothed; line search on M ensures descent if step small enough. Since we normalize gradient, step size O(1) may be too large; line search will reduce.

```
def _sanitize_sequence(seq, expected_len=None):
    if seq is None: return None
    try: iterable = list(seq)
    except: return None
    if not iterable: return None
    out=[]
    for x in iterable:
       if isinstance(x, bool): continue? We want valid candidates; use float conversion if finite else skip? For internal, can replace invalid with 0.
       try: v=float(x)
       except: v=0.0
       if math.isnan(v) or math.isinf(v): v=0.0
       if v<0: v=0.0
       if v>1000: v=1000.0
       out.append(v)
    a=np.array(out,float)
    if expected_len is not None and a.size != expected_len:
        # maybe resize to expected_len by truncating/padding
        if a.size < expected_len: pad zeros? Better not; return None or resize.
    if a.sum() <= 1e-12: return None
    return a
```

Creating starts for length n:
```
def _make_starts(n, seed, extra_candidates):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.ones(n))
    # skew exp
    i = np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
    for alpha in [0.5,1,2,4,8,16]:
       a=np.exp(alpha*i); starts.append(a)
       a=np.exp(-alpha*i) # same reversed maybe skip
    # two block
    cut=int(n*0.66)
    a=np.ones(n); a[:cut]*=2; starts.append(a)
    a=np.ones(n); a[cut:]*=2; starts.append(a)
    # random
    starts.append(rng.random(n)+1e-6)
    starts.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,n)))
    starts.append(np.exp(-np.abs(rng.normal(0,1,n))))
    # random Fourier
    for trial in range(3):
       a=np.ones(n)
       for k in range(1,5):
          f=rng.integers(1, max(2,n//4))
          phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          amp=rng.uniform(0.2,0.9)
          a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n + phase)
       a=np.clip(a,1e-8,None)
       starts.append(a)
    # sparse random support
    m=int(math.sqrt(n))*2
    for trial in range(2):
       a=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,m,size=True, replace=False); w=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,m)); a[idx]=w; starts.append(a)
    # global candidates for this n
    for c in extra_candidates:
       if len(c)==n: starts.append(np.asarray(c,float))
    return starts
```
Normalize each in optimize.

`_lse_optimize` details:
```
def _lse_optimize(a0, time_limit, seed, target_score, n_len=None):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n=a.size; L=nextpow2(2*n-1)
    M=internal_score(a); score=2*n*M
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=0.5; beta_factor=5.0
    stale=0; max_stale=2000; iter=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    fa=None; q=None
    while time.monotonic()<end:
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       q=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1].real
       q=np.maximum(q,0)
       M=q.max(); score=2*n*M
       if score < best_score*(1-1e-9): update best
       if score <= target_score: maybe break? but can continue if time.
       beta = max(1.0, beta_factor / max(M,1e-300))
       z = M - q
       # avoid overflow
       np.clip(z, 0, 1.0/beta?) Actually exp(-beta*z), z max0, min M-min. If beta*z >700, set 0.
       nz = np.minimum(beta*z, 700.0)
       r = np.exp(-nz); rsum=r.sum(); if rsum<=0: r[kmax]=1
       r /= rsum
       fr=np.fft.rfft(r, L)
       corr=np.fft.irfft(fr * np.conj(fa), L)[:n].real
       g=2*corr
       gc=g - np.mean(g)
       gs=math.sqrt(np.mean(gc*gc))
       if gs<1e-18:
          # random kick
          a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3,n)); a/=a.sum(); stale=0; continue
       step = eta * gc / gs
       np.clip(step, -30, 30, out=step)
       factor=np.exp(-step)
       a2=a*factor; s=a2.sum(); if s<=0 fail
       a2/=s
       # line search few? Since step normalized, evaluate once. If score worse, reduce eta.
       fa2=np.fft.rfft(a2,L); q2=irfft(fa2*fa2) ... M2
       if M2 < M*(1-1e-10):
          a=a2; M=M2; eta=min(eta*1.05,10); if score improved relative best: stale=0
          # improved exact maybe
       else:
          eta=max(eta*0.5,1e-6)
          stale +=1
          if stale>max_stale or iter%5000==0:
             a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.5, n)); a /= a.sum()
             # maybe block perturb
             stale=0; eta=0.5
       # beta_factor slowly increases
       if iter%500==0 and beta_factor<50: beta_factor*=1.1
       iter+=1
    # final exact? return best, internal bestscore
```

We can enforce by solving a 1D optimization changing variables in set S_k (pairs). This is like constraint programming. If a bin is violated, we can reduce all a_i involved and redistribute mass to low bins. Specifically, for max bin k, let S = {i: 0≤k-i<n}. The violation q_k. If we multiply all variables in S by factor λ<1, q_k reduces by λ² (if pairs within S? Actually q_k =∑_{i∈S} a_i a_{k-i}. Multiplying all a_i by λ for i∈S reduces each product by λ², so q_k→λ² q_k. Other coefficients affected. We can choose λ to bring q_k to target, and renormalize by increasing variables outside S. This is a block update. For multiple active bins, intersection. This resembles block perturbations but targeted. Could be effective.

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _now(): return time.monotonic()

def _pow2(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
Actually if m=2, (1).bit_length=1 ->2. Good.

`_normalize(a)`:
```
a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
s = a.sum()
if s <= 1e-12:
   return a + 1.0  # or None
a *= 1000.0 / s? No, sum1? For internal sum1; but return can be scaled. Use sum1.
return a / s
```

`_fft_q_and_fa(a,L)`:
```
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
q=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L).real
q = q[:2*a.size-1]
np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
return q, fa
```
Need q length may include small n; fine.

`_corr_r_a(r, fa, L, n)`:
```
fr = np.fft.rfft(r, L)
c = np.fft.irfft(fr * np.conj(fa), L).real[:n]
return 2*c
```
Should we real? yes.

`_softmax_weights(q, alpha)`:
```
M = q.max()
z = M - q
# alpha maybe already 1/M; if alpha<=0? 
z = np.minimum(z * alpha, 700.0)
w = np.exp(-z)
s = w.sum()
if not finite or s<=0:
   idx = int(np.argmax(q)); w[idx]=1; s=1
w /= s
return w
```
If alpha = beta_factor/M, then z*alpha = beta_factor*(M-q)/M; max0, min large. Good.

`_lse_run(a0, time_limit, seed, target_score, verbose=False)`:
Return best normalized array, internal best_score_fft.
```
def _lse_run(a0, time_limit, seed, target_score):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n==0: return None, inf
    L = _pow2(2*n -1)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = _fft_score(a,L)
    rng = ...
    eta = rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)
    alpha_factor = rng.uniform(2,20)
    stale=0; iter=0; max_stale=3000
    end = _now()+time_limit
    q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a,L)
    M=q.max(); score=2*n*M; best_score=score
    while _now()<end:
        # weights
        alpha = alpha_factor / max(M,1e-30)
        r = _softmax_weights(q, alpha)
        g = _corr_r_a(r, fa, L, n)
        gc = g - np.mean(g)
        gs = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(gc*gc)))
        if gs < 1e-15:
            # random kick
            if time left small break
            kick = rng.normal(0.0, rng.uniform(0.2,0.7), n)
            a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(kick), 1e-100)
            a /= a.sum()
            q,fa=...
            stale=0; iter+=1; eta=...
            continue
        step = eta * gc / gs
        np.clip(step, -25, 25, out=step)
        a2 = a * np.exp(-step)
        # floor
        a2 = np.maximum(a2, 1e-100)
        s2=a2.sum()
        ok = s2>0 and math.isfinite(s2)
        if not ok:
            eta*=0.5; stale+=1; ... continue
        a2 /= s2
        q2, fa2 = _fft_q_and_fa(a2,L)
        M2=q2.max(); score2=2*n*M2
        if math.isfinite(score2) and score2 < score * (1 - 1e-12):
            a=a2; q=q2; fa=fa2; M=M2; score=score2
            if score < best_score:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
                if verbose and random: print
            eta = min(eta*1.08, 8.0)
            stale=0
        else:
            eta = max(eta*0.5, 1e-5)
            stale += 1
            if stale > max_stale:
                kick = rng.normal(0.0, rng.uniform(0.5,1.2), n)
                a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(kick), 1e-100)
                a /= a.sum()
                q,fa=_fft_q_and_fa(a,L); M=q.max(); score=2*n*M
                stale=0; eta=rng.uniform(0.3,1.0); alpha_factor=rng.uniform(2,30)
        # adjust alpha factor occasionally
        if iter % 1000 == 0:
            if alpha_factor < 80 and rng.random()<0.7:
                alpha_factor *= 1.1
            elif alpha_factor > 2 and rng.random()<0.2:
                alpha_factor *= 0.8
        iter += 1
        if score < target_score and iter>5000: break? Could continue to improve. But for main, if very good, maybe break and polish separately. We can break if score <= 1.0? not.
    return best_a, best_score
```

def _direct_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return inf
   c=np.convolve(a,a)
   return 2*a.size*max(c)/(s*s)
```
Only use for final if evaluator not present.

```
def _lp_polish(a0, time_limit, seed):
    a = _normalize(a0); n=a.size
    if n<4 or n>512: return None
    try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except: return None
    L? not
    end=_now()+time_limit
    eta=0.5
    A_build each solve:
       A = np.zeros((n,2*n-1), dtype=np.float64)
       # We can construct via for i in range(n): A[i,i:i+n]=a
    best=a; best_score=direct? use fft.
    while time left:
       build A
       c=np.ones(2n-1)
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=-A, b_ub=-np.ones(n), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'dual_simplex': True, 'presolve': True, 'output_flag':0})
       if not res.success: break
       y=res.x
       v = A.dot(y)
       v = np.maximum(v,1e-12)
       # if v roughly constant
       vc = v - np.mean(v)
       if np.std(v) < 1e-8:
           random kick; A rebuild next
       else:
           step = eta * vc / np.std(v)
           np.clip(step,-25,25)
           a2=np.maximum(a*np.exp(-step),1e-100); a2/=a2.sum()
           check internal score; accept/reduce eta
    return best
```

Let's detail `_eval_as_array`:
```
def _score_sequence(list_or_array):
    seq = [float(x) for x in list_or_array]
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        val = None
    if val is not None:
        try:
            vf = float(val)
            if math.isfinite(vf): return vf
        except: pass
    # fallback direct
    a=np.asarray(list_or_array,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    return float(2*a.size*float(np.max(c))/(float(s)*float(s)))
```
Be careful if `evaluate_sequence` not defined, NameError caught. But referencing `evaluate_sequence` inside try okay.

Fallback direct with np.convolve for large n could be slow if evaluator missing; assume available.

Pseudo code final:
```
def _lse_run(a0, time_limit, seed, target_score):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    if a0 is None: return None, inf
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n < 1: return None, inf
    L = _pow2(2*n - 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = a0.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
    M = float(q.max())
    score = 2.0*n*M
    best_score = score
    if not math.isfinite(score): return best_a, inf
    end = _now() + max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    eta = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
    alpha_factor = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 20.0))
    stale = 0; it=0; max_stale=4000
    while _now() < end:
        if M <= 0: break
        alpha = alpha_factor / max(M, 1e-300)
        r = _softmax_weights(q, alpha)
        g = _corr_r_a(r, fa, L, n)
        gm = float(np.mean(g))
        gc = g - gm
        gs = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(gc*gc)))
        if not math.isfinite(gs) or gs < 1e-18:
            # kick
            kick_scale = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.8))
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, kick_scale, n)
            a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
            s = a.sum()
            if s <= 0: break
            a /= s
            q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
            M = float(q.max()); score=2*n*M
            stale=0; it+=1
            continue
        step = eta * gc / gs
        np.clip(step, -25.0, 25.0, out=step)
        a2 = a * np.exp(-step)
        a2 = np.maximum(a2, 1e-100)
        s2 = float(a2.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0:
            eta = max(eta*0.5, 1e-6); stale +=1
            if stale > max_stale: ...
            continue
        a2 /= s2
        q2, fa2 = _fft_q_and_fa(a2, L)
        M2 = float(q2.max()); score2=2*n*M2
        if math.isfinite(score2) and score2 < score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            a=a2; q=q2; fa=fa2; M=M2; score=score2
            if score < best_score:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
                if score < 1.50?: print? Maybe pass target.
            eta = min(eta * 1.05, 6.0)
            stale = 0
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.6, 1e-6)
            stale += 1
            if stale % 2000 == 0 or stale > max_stale:
                kick_scale = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.2))
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, kick_scale, n)
                # also maybe reverse if n? no
                a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
                # add small block perturb
                if rng.random() < 0.3:
                   bl = max(1, n//32)
                   st = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                   a[st:min(n,st+bl)] *= np.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0,0.3)))
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-100)
                s = a.sum()
                if s > 0:
                    q,fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a,L); M=float(q.max()); score=2*n*M
                    a /= s? Need normalize before q? Actually a sum changed; should normalize before q. Do:
                a /= s; q,fa=...
                stale=0; eta=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)); alpha_factor=float(rng.uniform(2,30))
        # schedule alpha
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            if alpha_factor < 60.0 and (rng.random() < 0.8 or score > 2.0): alpha_factor *= 1.1
            elif alpha_factor > 2.0: alpha_factor *= 0.9
        it += 1
        if best_score < target_score and it > 20000:
            # could stop for this run to let main explore others, but maybe continue small? We stop to save time.
            break
    return best_a, best_score
```

Let R length L, A length L. Define C = ifft(DFT(R) * conj(DFT(A))). In numpy, ifft uses 1/N sum X_k e^{2πi kn/N}. DFT R_k=∑_j R_j e^{-2πi jk/N}. Product inverse:
C_n = 1/N ∑_k (∑_j R_j e^{-2πi jk/N})(∑_m A_m e^{2πi mk/N}) e^{2πi nk/N}
=∑_j R_j (1/N∑_k e^{2πi (n + m -j)k/N}) ∑_m? Wait A factor m? Actually conj(DFT(A))_k=∑_m A_m e^{2πi mk/N}. Sum =∑_{j,m} R_j A_m δ_{n-m+j?} Need solve e^{2πi (n + m - j)k/N}; delta n + m - j ≡0 => j ≡ n + m. So C_n=∑_m R_{n+m mod L} A_m. Desired g_m? For variable i=n, t=m, R[i+t] A[t]. Yes C_i=∑_t R_{i+t} A_t. Good. Great.

For q, DFT convolution: ifft(DFT(a)*DFT(a)) = a*a (circular), with index k=∑ a_i a_j where i+j ≡k. Since L>=2n-1, linear.

Now, the gradient of q_k = ∑_i a_i a_{k-i}; derivative wrt a_i: For a fixed i, terms where i and k-i. The sum over k r_k a_{k-i} gives each pair product twice except diagonal? Let's verify derivative of ∑_k r_k ∑_i a_i a_{k-i}. For a_i, if k-i=i => k=2i, term a_i² derivative 2a_i; formula 2∑r_k a_{k-i} includes 2r_{2i}a_i, correct. For off-diagonal, term appears with i and i' in two k? Actually derivative by chain rule: d q_k / da_i = a_{k-i} (if valid) + a_{i?} from the other a? But q_k as ∑_i a_i a_{k-i}; when differentiating, the first factor gives a_{k-i}; the second factor: q_k also =∑_i a_{k-i} a_i, same sum, so derivative from second factor is a_{k-i} again? More formally q_k =∑_i a_i b_i with b_i=a_{k-i} (b depends on a). ∂q_k/∂a_j = b_j + ∑_i a_i ∂b_i/∂a_j. b_i = a_{k-i}; ∂b_i/∂a_j is 1 if k-i=j => i=k-j. So second term = a_{k-j}. Thus total = a_{k-j}+a_{k-j}=2a_{k-j}. Good. For j=k-j (2j=k), first b_j=a_j, second a_j =>2a_j, correct. So gradient 2 corr. Good.

`_normalize_arr`:
```
def _normalize_arr(arr):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s
```
If arr contains object? dtype conversion may fail; return None.

```
def _to_array(seq):
    try:
       lst = list(seq)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if not lst: return None
    vals=[]
    for x in lst:
       if isinstance(x, bool):
          continue? For internal, we can set 0.
       try: v=float(x)
       except Exception: v=0.0
       if not math.isfinite(v): v=0.0
       if v<0: v=0.0
       elif v>1000: v=1000.0
       vals.append(v)
    a=np.array(vals,float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return None
    return a
```

`_make_starts`:
Need `extra` list of arrays, `n` int, `seed` int.
```
def _make_starts(n, seed, extra):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=[]
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=float) / max(1, n-1)
    h=None
    for arr in extra:
       if int(arr.size)==n:
          h=np.asarray(arr,float); break
    if h is not None:
       h=_normalize_arr(h)
       if h is not None:
          starts.append(h)
          for alpha in [1.0,3.0,10.0]:
             starts.append(h*np.exp(alpha*x))
          starts.append(h*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.3,n)))
    # ones last maybe
    starts.append(np.ones(n,dtype=float))
    for alpha in [0.5,1,2,5,10,20]:
       starts.append(np.exp(alpha*x))
       starts.append(np.exp(-alpha*x))
    # piecewise
    for K in [2,3,4,6]:
       cuts=np.sort(rng.integers(1,n-1,K-1,size=K-1)) if K>1 else []
       a=np.empty(n)
       bounds=[0]+list(cuts)+[n]
       for segment in range(K):
          val=float(rng.uniform(0.5,2.5))
          a[bounds[segment]:bounds[segment+1]]=val
       starts.append(a)
    # randoms
    starts.append(rng.random(n)+1e-6)
    starts.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)))
    starts.append(np.exp(-np.abs(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n))))
    # Gaussians
    for mu in [0.15,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.85]:
       for alpha in [5,15,40]:
          starts.append(np.exp(-alpha*(x-mu)**2))
    # Fourier
    for _ in range(3):
       a=np.ones(n)
       for _2 in range(4):
          f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//5)))
          amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0))
          phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+phase)
       starts.append(np.maximum(a,1e-6))
    # sparse
    for _ in range(2):
       m=int(max(4, min(n, n**0.5 * 2)))
       idx=rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
       a=np.zeros(n)
       a[idx]=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,m))
       starts.append(a)
    return starts
```

def _try_trim(a):
   a=a.copy()
   tol=1e-14
   idx=np.flatnonzero(a>tol)
   if idx.size==0: return None
   b=a[idx[0]:idx[-1]+1]
   if b.size < a.size:
       s=b.sum()
       if s>0: b/=s
       return b
   return None
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2)

using a familie of fast FFT-based smoothed-L∞ descent heuristics, multi-start
restarts, optional small-n LP polishing (via the dual covering LP), and final
selection by the authoritative evaluate_sequence() when available.

The objective is scale invariant, so all internal sequences are normalized to
unit sum.  A safe 2-length optimum [2/3, 1/3] is always available, giving the
exact score 16/9 ≈ 1.777..., and the routine always returns the best exact
candidate found before the deadline.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _now():
    return time.monotonic()


def _pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_arr(arr):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = a.copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _to_array(seq):
    try:
        lst = list(seq)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if not lst:
        return None
    vals = []
    for x in lst:
        if isinstance(x, bool):
            vals.append(0.0)
            continue
        try:
            v = float(x)
        except Exception:
            v = 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(v):
            v = 0.0
        if v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        elif v > 1000.0:
            v = 1000.0
        vals.append(v)
    a = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.sum() <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a


def _fft_q_and_fa(a, L):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L).real
    n = int(a.size)
    q = q[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return q, fa


def _fft_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    q, _ = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
    M = float(q.max())
    return 2.0 * float(n) * M


def _softmax_weights(q, alpha):
    M = float(q.max())
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(q)
        return w
    z = M - q
    z = np.minimum(z * alpha, 700.0)
    w = np.exp(-z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(q)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def _corr_r_a(r, fa, L, n):
    fr = np.fft.rfft(r, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fr * np.conj(fa), L).real
    return 2.0 * c[:n]


def _try_trim(a, tol=1e-14):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    idx = np.flatnonzero(a > tol)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return None
    b = a[int(idx[0]):int(idx[-1]) + 1]
    if b.size == 0:
        return None
    if b.size < a.size:
        s = float(b.sum())
        if math.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            b = b / s
    return b


def _safe_eval(seq):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in seq]
    except Exception:
        vals = []
    if not vals:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(vals)
    except Exception:
        val = None
    if val is not None:
        try:
            vf = float(val)
            if math.isfinite(vf):
                return vf
        except Exception:
            pass
    a = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        return float(2.0 * a.size * cmax / (s * s))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _make_starts(n, seed, extra):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = int(n)
    starts = []
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)

    h = None
    for arr in extra:
        if int(arr.size) == n:
            h = _normalize_arr(arr)
            if h is not None:
                break
    if h is not None:
        starts.append(h)
        for alpha in (1.0, 3.0, 10.0):
            starts.append(h * np.exp(alpha * x))
            starts.append(h * np.exp(alpha * (1.0 - x)))
        starts.append(h * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, n)))

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0):
        starts.append(np.exp(alpha * x))
        starts.append(np.exp(alpha * (1.0 - x)))

    for c in (2.0, 4.0, 5.0, 6.0, 8.0, 12.0, 20.0):
        starts.append(1.0 / (1.0 + c * x))
        starts.append(1.0 / (1.0 + c * x) ** 2)
        starts.append(1.0 / (1.0 + c * (1.0 - x)))

    for K in (2, 3, 4, 6):
        if K <= 1:
            continue
        cuts = np.sort(rng.integers(1, n, size=K - 1))
        bounds = [0] + [int(c) for c in cuts] + [n]
        a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for s in range(K):
            val = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 2.5))
            a[bounds[s]:bounds[s + 1]] = val
        starts.append(a)

    for mu in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 0.9):
        for alpha in (4.0, 10.0, 25.0, 60.0):
            starts.append(np.exp(-alpha * (x - mu) * (x - mu)))

    starts.append(rng.random(n) + 1e-6)
    starts.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)))
    starts.append(np.exp(-np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))))

    for _ in range(3):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _2 in range(4):
            fmax = max(2, n // 5)
            f = int(rng.integers(1, fmax))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        starts.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-6))

    for _ in range(2):
        m = int(max(4, min(n, 2.0 * math.sqrt(n))))
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[idx] = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m))
        starts.append(a)

    return starts


def _lse_run(a0, time_limit, seed, stop_score=None):
    a = _normalize_arr(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2:
        return a, 2.0
    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_a = a.copy()
    q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
    M = float(q.max())
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * M
    best_score = score

    if not math.isfinite(score):
        return best_a, float("inf")

    end = _now() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    eta = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
    alpha_factor = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 20.0))
    stale = 0
    it = 0
    max_stale = 5000

    while _now() < end:
        if M <= 0.0:
            break
        alpha = alpha_factor / max(M, 1e-300)
        r = _softmax_weights(q, alpha)
        g = _corr_r_a(r, fa, L, n)
        gm = float(np.mean(g))
        gc = g - gm
        gs = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(gc * gc)))

        if not math.isfinite(gs) or gs < 1e-18:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.8)), n)
            a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if math.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                a /= s
                q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
                M = float(q.max())
                score = 2.0 * float(n) * M
                if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_a = a.copy()
            stale = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        step = eta * gc / gs
        np.clip(step, -25.0, 25.0, out=step)
        a2 = a * np.exp(-step)
        a2 = np.maximum(a2, 1e-100)
        s2 = float(a2.sum())

        if not math.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
            eta = max(eta * 0.5, 1e-6)
            stale += 1
            if stale > max_stale:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.6, 1.2)), n)
                a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if math.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                    a /= s
                    q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
                    M = float(q.max())
                    score = 2.0 * float(n) * M
                stale = 0
                eta = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
                alpha_factor = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 30.0))
            continue

        a2 = a2 / s2
        q2, fa2 = _fft_q_and_fa(a2, L)
        M2 = float(q2.max())
        score2 = 2.0 * float(n) * M2

        if math.isfinite(score2) and score2 < score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            a = a2
            q = q2
            fa = fa2
            M = M2
            score = score2
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
            eta = min(eta * 1.05, 6.0)
            stale = 0
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.6, 1e-6)
            stale += 1
            if stale > max_stale or (stale > 0 and stale % 2000 == 0):
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.6, 1.3)), n)
                a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
                if rng.random() < 0.35:
                    bl = max(1, n // 40)
                    st = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                    a[st:min(n, st + bl)] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35)))
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-100)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if math.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                    a /= s
                    q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
                    M = float(q.max())
                    score = 2.0 * float(n) * M
                    if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best_a = a.copy()
                stale = 0
                eta = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
                alpha_factor = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 30.0))

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            if alpha_factor < 80.0 and rng.random() < 0.8:
                alpha_factor *= 1.1
            elif alpha_factor > 2.0:
                alpha_factor *= 0.9

        it += 1
        if stop_score is not None and math.isfinite(stop_score) and best_score <= stop_score * 0.985 and it > 30000:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def _lp_polish(a0, time_limit, seed):
    a = _normalize_arr(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 4 or n > 400:
        return a, _fft_score(a)

    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
        return a, _fft_score(a)

    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    best_a = a.copy()
    q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(q.max())

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    end = _now() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    eta = 0.5
    solves = 0
    max_solves = 35
    stale = 0

    while _now() < end and solves < max_solves:
        A = np.zeros((n, 2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            A[i, i:i + n] = a

        try:
            c = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            res = linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=-A,
                b_ub=-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=(0.0, None),
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        solves += 1
        y = None
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
            y = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if y is None:
                y = None

        if y is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            if stale > 6:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
                a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
                a = a / a.sum()
                q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
                cur = 2.0 * float(n) * float(q.max())
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
            break

        v = A.dot(y)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            break

        vc = v - np.median(v)
        sv = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(vc * vc)))
        if not math.isfinite(sv) or sv < 1e-14:
            stale += 1
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
            a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
            a = a / a.sum()
            q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
            cur = 2.0 * float(n) * float(q.max())
            if cur < best_score:
                best_score = cur
                best_a = a.copy()
            continue

        step = eta * vc / sv
        np.clip(step, -25.0, 25.0, out=step)
        a2 = np.maximum(a * np.exp(-step), 1e-100)
        s2 = float(a2.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
            eta *= 0.5
            continue
        a2 = a2 / s2
        q2, fa2 = _fft_q_and_fa(a2, L)
        sc2 = 2.0 * float(n) * float(q2.max())
        if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            a = a2
            q, fa = q2, fa2
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = a.copy()
            eta = min(eta * 1.1, 4.0)
            stale = 0
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.6, 1e-4)
            stale += 1
            if stale > 3:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
                a = np.maximum(a * np.exp(noise), 1e-100)
                a = a / a.sum()
                q, fa = _fft_q_and_fa(a, L)
                cur = 2.0 * float(n) * float(q.max())
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
                stale = 0

    return best_a, best_score


def _raw_score_candidates(n, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)
    cands = []
    for c in (3.0, 4.0, 5.0, 6.0, 8.0, 10.0):
        cands.append(1.0 / (1.0 + c * x))
        cands.append(1.0 / (1.0 + c * x) ** 2)
    for c in (1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0):
        cands.append(np.exp(-c * x))

    for arr in cands:
        if _now() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            a = _normalize_arr(arr)
            if a is None:
                continue
            b = _try_trim(a)
            if b is not None:
                yield b
        except Exception:
            continue
        yield a


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t0 = _now()

    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    if "budget_s" in kwargs and kwargs["budget_s"] is not None:
        try:
            total_budget = float(kwargs["budget_s"])
        except Exception:
            pass

    deadline = None
    for key in ("deadline", "dead_line", "end_time", "absolute_deadline", "deadline_s"):
        if key in kwargs and kwargs[key] is not None:
            try:
                deadline = float(kwargs[key])
                break
            except Exception:
                pass

    if deadline is None:
        deadline = t0 + total_budget
    deadline = max(t0 + 20.0, deadline - 8.0)

    try:
        seed = int(kwargs.get("seed", seed))
    except Exception:
        seed = 12345

    extra = []
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        arr = _to_array(hs)
        if arr is not None and 2 <= int(arr.size) <= 8000:
            extra.append(arr)
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_a = np.array([2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    best_score = _safe_eval(best_a.tolist())
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 16.0 / 9.0

    base_len = 5582
    if extra:
        base_len = int(extra[0].size)
    if not (2 <= base_len <= 8000):
        base_len = 5582

    lengths = []
    if 4 <= base_len <= 8000:
        lengths.append(int(base_len))
    for L in (5582, 2048, 1024, 512, 256):
        if int(L) not in lengths:
            lengths.append(int(L))
    lengths = [L for L in lengths if 4 <= L <= 8000][:5]

    total_time = max(0.0, deadline - _now() - 12.0)
    if total_time > 60.0:
        raw_len = min(lengths[0], 6000)
        for cand in _raw_score_candidates(raw_len, deadline):
            if _now() > deadline - 12.0:
                break
            try:
                sc = _safe_eval(cand.tolist())
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
            except Exception:
                pass

    fracs = [0.55, 0.20, 0.12, 0.08, 0.05]
    fsum = float(sum(fracs))

    for idx, n in enumerate(lengths):
        if _now() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        frac = fracs[idx] / fsum if idx < len(fracs) else 0.05 / fsum
        len_time = total_time * frac

        if n < 4:
            n = 64

        starts = _make_starts(n, seed + 999 * (idx + 1), extra)
        min_slice = 25.0
        max_starts = max(1, int(len_time / min_slice))
        starts = starts[:max_starts]
        if not starts:
            continue

        per_start = float(len_time) / float(len(starts))
        cap = 900.0 if n <= 512 else (450.0 if n <= 2048 else 200.0)

        for j, st in enumerate(starts):
            remaining = deadline - _now() - 8.0
            if remaining < 18.0:
                break
            slice_time = min(per_start, remaining / max(1, len(starts) - j) * 0.95, cap)
            if slice_time < 10.0:
                break

            try:
                a_best, sc_fft = _lse_run(st, slice_time, seed * 1000003 + 31 * idx + j)
            except Exception:
                a_best, sc_fft = None, float("inf")

            if a_best is None:
                continue

            candidates = [np.asarray(a_best, dtype=np.float64)]
            tr = _try_trim(candidates[0])
            if tr is not None:
                candidates.append(np.asarray(tr, dtype=np.float64))

            for cand in candidates:
                if cand.size < 2:
                    continue
                try:
                    sc = _safe_eval(cand.tolist())
                except Exception:
                    sc = float("inf")
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)

            if n <= 400 and _now() < deadline - 80.0:
                try:
                    ap, s_pol = _lp_polish(candidates[0], min(80.0, deadline - _now() - 10.0),
                                           seed * 777 + idx * 13 + j)
                    if ap is not None:
                        trp = _try_trim(ap)
                        for cand in [ap, trp] if trp is not None else [ap]:
                            if cand is None or cand.size < 2:
                                continue
                            sc = _safe_eval(cand.tolist())
                            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                                best_score = sc
                                best_a = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    final_time = max(0.0, deadline - _now() - 4.0)
    if final_time > 10.0 and best_a is not None:
        try:
            nl = int(best_a.size)
            if 4 <= nl <= 8000:
                a_pol, _ = _lse_run(best_a, min(60.0, final_time), seed * 9999839 + 17)
                if a_pol is not None:
                    cands = [a_pol, _try_trim(a_pol)]
                    for cand in cands:
                        if cand is None or cand.size < 2:
                            continue
                        sc = _safe_eval(cand.tolist())
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = sc
                            best_a = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        out = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
    except Exception:
        out = [float(x) for x in [2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0]]

    if not out:
        out = [float(2.0 / 3.0), float(1.0 / 3.0)]

    return out
```
